- **Rama-pandita Returns, Rules, and Weds His Sister-Queen** — After the exile term and the widow-queen's death, Rama-pandita returns to rule and takes his sister Sita as consecrated chief queen — the Buddhist sibling-couple inversion _(actors: Rama-pandita, Sita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/jain-and-buddhist-ramayanas.md) — (Dasaratha Jataka (No. 461))
- **Buddha Reveals the Bodhisatta Identifications** — In the closing samodhana the Buddha reveals Rama-pandita was the Bodhisatta, Lakkhana was Sariputta, and the other characters his past-life associates _(actors: Buddha, Rama-pandita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/jain-and-buddhist-ramayanas.md) — (Dasaratha Jataka (No. 461))
- **Shiva destroys Tripura** — Shiva destroys the three flying cities (Tripura) of the asuras Tarakaksha, Vidyunmali and Kamalaksha with a single arrow _(actors: Shiva, Tarakaksha, Vidyunmali, Kamalaksha)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/devas-asuras-and-the-lokas.md) — (Shiva Purana; Mahabharata)
- **The Vālakhilyas' penance births Garuḍa** — Mocked by Indra at Kaśyapa's sacrifice, the thumb-sized Vālakhilya sages perform penance to create a rival Indra, the boon ripening as the mighty Garuḍa _(actors: Vālakhilyas, Indra, Kaśyapa, Garuḍa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/vidyadharas-siddhas-and-spirits.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **The Siddha instructs the Brahmana of the Kashyapa line** — In the Anugita's opening embedded story, a wandering perfected being teaches a troubled Brahmana about transmigration and release, likening the soul entering a body to molten iron poured into a mould _(actors: Siddha, Kashyapa Brahmana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/anugita.md) — (Mahabharata 14, Anugita (Telang trans.))
- **The Brahmana teaches his wife (the Brahmana-gita)** — A sage's wife finds her husband in meditative seclusion and questions him on the attainable worlds, sacrifice, and liberation, eliciting the inner-fire allegory of the agnihotra within the body _(actors: Brahmana sage, his wife)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/anugita.md) — (Mahabharata, Anugita (Telang trans.))
- **The guru instructs the disciple on Samkhya tattvas** — In the closing Anugita dialogue an enlightened teacher answers a disciple's questions on the highest truth, expounding the three gunas and the Samkhya enumeration of tattvas up to liberation _(actors: enlightened teacher, disciple)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/anugita.md) — (Mahabharata, Anugita (Telang trans.))
- **Markandeya's witness of the pralaya flood** — Granted vision of the dissolution, the sage Markandeya sees the cosmic flood and the infant Krishna floating on a banyan leaf, the narrative anchor for what persists through pralaya _(actors: Markandeya, infant Krishna/Narayana)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/kalpa-and-pralaya.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 12.8-10; Mahabharata Vana Parva)
- **The dialogue of the adhvaryu priest and the ascetic** — An officiating adhvaryu priest and an ascetic debate whether internal concentration outranks outer sacrificial slaughter, touching on ahimsa and the ethics of ritual killing _(actors: adhvaryu priest, ascetic)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/anugita.md) — (Anugita (Telang trans.))
- **Harikeśa banished and adopted by Śiva as guardian of Kāśī** — The yakṣa Harikeśa, banished for worshipping Śiva against his father's Kubera-devotion, is adopted by Śiva as an attendant and set as guardian of Kāśī _(actors: Harikeśa, Śiva, Pūrṇabhadra)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/yakshas-and-guhyakas.md) — (Matsya Purāṇa 18; Śiva Purāṇa)
- **Shiva burns Kama to ash (Kamadahana)** — Shiva burns Kama, the love-god, to ash with his third eye, commemorated in the South as Kama Dahanam, devotion triumphing over desire _(actors: Shiva, Kama)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Shiva myth cycle)
- **Lingodbhava - appearance of the infinite jyotirlinga** — Shiva appears as an infinite pillar of light (jyotirlinga) whose top and bottom Brahma and Vishnu cannot find, basis of Maha Shivaratri and the 12 Jyotirlingas _(actors: Shiva, Brahma, Vishnu)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Linga Purana)
- **Narasimha slays Hiranyakashipu (Narasimha Jayanti)** — Narasimha, the man-lion 4th avatar, appears to slay Hiranyakashipu and save Prahlada, commemorated on Vaishakha Shukla Chaturdashi _(actors: Narasimha, Hiranyakashipu, Prahlada)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Bhagavata Purana)
- **Birth of Ganesha and his beheading and restoration** — Parvati forms Ganesha from her body's paste; Shiva beheads the boy guarding her door and restores him with an elephant's head; commemorated at Ganesh Chaturthi _(actors: Parvati, Shiva, Ganesha)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Ganesha & Kartikeya cycle)
- **Kartikeya slays Surapadma (Skanda Shashthi)** — Kartikeya/Murugan slays the asura Surapadma, commemorated by the Skanda Shashthi festival especially in Tamil Nadu _(actors: Kartikeya, Surapadma)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Ganesha & Kartikeya cycle)
- **Goddess Sankranti slays Sankarasura** — The goddess Sankranti slays the demon Sankarasura in a Puranic legend tied to Makar Sankranti _(actors: Sankranti, Sankarasura)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Puranic)
- **Indrani ties a raksha thread on Indra** — Indrani ties a sacred thread on Indra before his war with the asuras to ensure victory, an early raksha charter _(actors: Indrani, Indra)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Puranic)
- **Savitri compels Yama to restore Satyavan (Vat Savitri)** — Savitri compels Yama to restore the life of her husband Satyavan, the upakhyana enacted by married women at Vat Savitri _(actors: Savitri, Yama, Satyavan)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva (Savitri-Satyavan))
- **Ekadashi born to slay the demon Mura** — The Padma Purana personifies Ekadashi as a power born to slay the demon Mura, charter of the Ekadashi vrata _(actors: Ekadashi, Mura)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Padma Purana)
